[ios/web_view] Add prewarm support to ios_web_view_shell

Adds a way to exercise -[CWVWebView prewarm] manually:
1. A "Recreate and prewarm web view" menu item that replaces the current
   web view with a new one and calls -prewarm on it.
2. A "Recreate and load about:blank" menu item that replaces the current
   web view with a new one and loads about:blank in it, the way an
   embedder keeps a web view ready without -prewarm. Comparing the two
   shows the difference: after about:blank, the next page is the second
   back/forward item and Back returns to about:blank; after -prewarm, it
   is the first item.
3. After each address bar load, logs the time from the load request to
   commit and to finish, plus the back/forward list counts, canGoBack,
   and history.length.

Bug: 584753593

[ios/web_view] Add -[CWVWebView prewarm]

Embedders that keep a CWVWebView ready today load about:blank, which
leaves a blank item at index 0 of the back/forward list, so
history.back() returns to a blank page. The window.open() initializer
avoids that but resets the BrowserState wide configuration provider.

-prewarm calls the new web::PrewarmWebView(), which creates the
WKWebView through the normal first navigation path, without resetting
the provider. No navigation item is created. It does nothing once a
load has started, so it is safe to call repeatedly.

A user agent set after -prewarm still applies to the first load
(covered by new ios_web_view_inttests).

On an iPhone 13 Pro Max, median load to commit dropped from about 275
ms to about 188 ms (en.wikipedia.org, 10 runs each), and history.length
stayed 1. Also running an empty script to start the web content process
early made no measurable difference, so -prewarm only creates the web
view.

Bug: 584753593

3f3f401e8489717f89850b262d5c36525e343530
parent
10ec29d71e50de5222f137a925be5e33efab3d30 [ diff ] 
[glic] Resolve active BrowserWindowInterface dynamically for tools

GlicWebClientHandler owns GlicToolsHolder across Browser window
lifetimes (for example, when Glic is detached in a floating window).
Previously, CreateAiOverlayToolsForGlic resolved active_browser once
at Mojo pipe creation and stored a raw_ptr<BrowserWindowInterface> on
AiOverlayTools, triggering a MiraclePtr DanglingPtr crash if that
initial Browser window closed while Glic remained open.

Add a BrowserResolver overload to AiOverlayTools::Create() (and
subscribe to RegisterBrowserDidClose() for fixed-browser callers) so
Glic dynamically resolves the most recently activated normal
BrowserWindowInterface for the Profile on each tool call.

Bug: 587935179
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=*GlicChromeTools*

Sun Nov 22 00:51:34 2026
tree
3d5b544f696123cd750d5bcdcc9a8a2795b0fe8e
parent
a6d88abeb139bd14abb954c6e783c4b2e74883dd [ diff ] 
google_apis: redact api key and secret values from vlog

CalculateKeyValue was logging raw API keys and OAuth client secrets to
VLOG(1) when loaded via env vars, gaia config, CLI switches, or
default_if_unset. Anyone running with `--enable-logging --v=1` (common
in bug reports) ended up with cleartext credentials in their logs.

Info.plist and feature overrides already skipped logging values. Now the
three runtime overrides log a redacted preview via `RedactKeyValue()`
(prefix/suffix + length for >=16 printable ASCII chars, or
`[empty]`/`[redacted, len=N]`) so devs can still tell overrides apart,
while `default_if_unset` omits the value altogether.

Bug: None
Change-Id: Ic4e1c6fc8cfb67b5b045d80b9bb61ccfaa0d985b

0093810caa9ac6b28033aa0ff75422579c933165
parent
8b81b59fc6586baeabc7f8a432e48f2c3ae30880 [ diff ] 
[Clipboard] Handle null streams from drag sources

A drag source can return TYMED_ISTREAM without an IStream pointer,
causing a CHECK failure when Chrome reads its file contents.

Treat the missing stream as a failed read and mark the medium TYMED_NULL
before releasing it. Preserve the existing filename handling and
valid-stream behavior.

Fixed: 583454016

Remove profile country access for PUMA

PUMA has been discontinued and PumaService, the only user of
MetricsServiceClient::GetProfileCountryIdForPrivateMetricsReporting()
and of CountryAccessReason::kPrivateUserMetricsReporting, has been
deleted. Remove both, along with the PumaService friend declaration in
CountryAccessKey and ChromeMetricsServiceClient::cached_profile_, which
was only used to look up the profile country.

tree
16b3721df41a5b519ef3c23e155afe235275fec4
parent
23c64e9eb7939c0571c952dc5dcbe5c8fde37aa5 [ diff ] 
[GEiC] Mock PrefsBrowserProxy in SettingsTest.GeicSubpage

GeicSubpageTest used the real chrome.settingsPrivate backend instead of
TestPrefsBrowserProxy. In DefaultTabAccessTogglePref, calling
setPrefValue(DEFAULT_TAB_CONTEXT_ENABLED, false) updates the backend
pref from its default true to false; SettingsPrivateEventRouter posts an
asynchronous SendPrefChange task while setPref() resolves immediately.
When that delayed onPrefsChanged(false) event arrives after
toggle.$.control.click() sets the local PrefService cache back to true,
it overwrites the cached pref with false and flakes the assertion.

Configure TestPrefsBrowserProxy and reset PrefService in GeicSubpageTest
setup, matching the other Lit Settings unit tests.

Test history:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chrome/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C%3Abrowser_tests%21gtest%3A%3ASettingsTest%23GeicSubpage

Bug: 580711828, 580721502

[Theme] Rename isNtpThemeCustomizationEnabled() to ...ByPolicy()

This is a renaming CL without any behaviour change.

After removing the NewTabPageCustomizationV2 feature flag, the 0-arg
NtpCustomizationUtils.isNtpThemeCustomizationEnabled() overload only
checks the enterprise policy, while the 2-arg overload checks device and edge-to-edge support as well.

In this CL:
- Rename the 0-arg
  NtpCustomizationUtils.isNtpThemeCustomizationEnabled() method to
  isNtpThemeCustomizationEnabledByPolicy() and update all callers so
  the distinction from the 2-arg overload is explicit.

019b4a00b92db4d0ebc39dea450a017f2bff40c7 [ diff ] 
[Theme] Clean up feature flag NewTabPageCustomizationV2

NewTabPageCustomizationV2 has been enabled by default and fully
launched, and its debug params now live on
NewTabPageCustomizationThemeDebug, so the flag only adds checks that are
always true.

In this CL:
- Remove the NewTabPageCustomizationV2 feature, its Java CachedFlag and
  all of its isEnabled() checks; NtpCustomizationUtils.
  isNtpThemeCustomizationEnabled() now only checks the enterprise policy
  and isNTPCustomizationSyncEnabled() only checks
  NewTabPageCustomizationThemeSync.
- Replace the duplicated feature-state fields in FeedSurfaceCoordinator
  with a constructor-local isNtpThemeCustomizationEnabled variable, and
  rename the misnamed V2 fields and locals in ToolbarPositionController
  and TabbedRootUiCoordinator.
- Replace the tests of the flag-disabled state with policy-disabled
  tests where no such test existed yet.

[TTC] Prompt for Gemini API key in WebUI when unset

Now that the built-in default API key has been removed from Talk to
Chrome, opening the overlay in non-MES mode without a CLI flag or bundle
key fails during startup. Add an inline API key input in the overlay
WebUI so users can paste and persist their own Gemini API key locally:

- Persist user-entered API keys in `window.localStorage` under
  `ttc_gemini_api_key` on `chrome-untrusted://ai-overlay-dialog`.
- Preserve auth precedence in non-MES mode: CLI `loadTimeData` `apiKey`
  (`AiOverlayDialog:api_key/...`), then saved WebUI key, then custom
  bundle `apiConfig.apiKey`, and include the saved key in `hasLocalAuth`
  for built-in bundle fallback.
- Enter `InitializationState.NEEDS_API_KEY` when no key is available and
  render a password input and Save button inside the overlay.
- Keep `InitializationState.ERROR` on startup failures instead of
  resetting to `UNINITIALIZED` in `stopConversation()`, and expose Retry
  and Change API key actions so invalid or expired saved keys can be
  replaced.
- Add Mocha and browsertest coverage in `ai_overlay_dialog` for prompt
  rendering, `localStorage` persistence across remounts and navigations,
  CLI override bypass, and error recovery.

[DevTools] Fix worker attachment and startup races in worker-console.js
📋 Metadata

3f5d7c55acb248482cc3f03243a4565f3fdacc3d
[ log ]
author

Sat Nov 21 20:31:15 2026

Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Nov 21 20:31:15 2026
tree
011095c4338b95224531c5bc5ef42f260f14c020
parent
cd15ec2a7649bfc5de1b33dfe79daaed03b1e380 [ diff ] 
[DevTools] Fix worker attachment and startup races in worker-console.js

inspector-protocol/worker/worker-console.js had async races across its
worker lifecycle sections:

1. Section 2 called worker.terminate() without waiting for the worker
   target to be destroyed in the browser, and section 3 registered
   dp.Target.onceAttachedToTarget() before
   Target.setAutoAttach({autoAttach: true}). Because worker termination
   in the browser is asynchronous, setAutoAttach frequently re-attached
   to section 2's dying worker, resolving attachedPromise with a stale
   session that immediately detached and causing Runtime.enable() to
   fail with "Session with given id not found".
2. Sections 3 and 4 called session.evaluate('startWorker()') without
   awaiting the worker's initial "ready" message, allowing subsequent
   postToWorker() calls to overwrite worker.onmessage before "ready"
   arrived, terminated auto-attached workers without awaiting
   Target.detachedFromTarget, and left the final
   Target.setAutoAttach({autoAttach: false}) unawaited.

Await Target.onceTargetDestroyed() when terminating section 2's worker,
register onceAttachedToTarget() after setAutoAttach and await it
together with startWorker() via Promise.all in sections 3 and 4, await
Target.onceDetachedFromTarget() (filtered by sessionId) when terminating
auto-attached workers, and await the final Target.setAutoAttach() call.

Test history:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2F%5C%3Ablink_web_tests%21webtest%3A%3Ainspector-protocol%2Fworker%23worker-console.js

Bug: None

parent
cfa8a707a73fcf8bb6a34f81e18aaf9fcb2023dd [ diff ] 
[webauthn] Add a request controller and discovery

Introduce GpmBrowserAuthenticatorDiscovery and
GpmBrowserAuthenticatorController to wire up GpmBrowserAuthenticator to
request dispatch and the UI.

- GpmBrowserAuthenticatorDiscovery provides a single-use callback that
  instantiates a GpmBrowserAuthenticator and notifies the FIDO request
  handler when the user selects a GPM passkey or confirms creation.
- GpmBrowserAuthenticatorController fetches passkeys security domain
  secrets from TrustedVaultClient, queries the security domain
  registration state via TrustedVaultConnection, drives the
  AuthenticatorRequestDialogModel steps (passkey creation confirmation,
  passkey selection, and MagicArch security domain recovery), and
  persists newly created passkeys to PasskeyModel.

Bug: 570170982

omnibox: Migrate OmniboxSuggestionButtonRowView buttons to ui::ColorId

Following crrev.com/c/8692528, `views::MdTextButton` supports
`ui::ColorVariant` for background and stroke overrides in addition to
text and vector-icon `ui::ColorId`s.

Pass `ui::ColorId`s directly to `OmniboxSuggestionRowChip` and
`OmniboxSuggestionRowButton` instead of resolving `SkColor`s in
`OnThemeChanged()` and `UpdateBackgroundColor()`, and remove the now
redundant overrides and unused chip theme state.

Screenshot: https://screenshot.googleplex.com/x5vdvsSZNKKtNJz
Bug: 394420459

Disable Metal shader cache in GPU tests

Adds --disable-metal-shader-cache to the arguments passed in to GPU
integration tests when running on Mac with Apple Silicon. This is to
temporarily work around a bug in the interaction between the shader
cache and the partition allocator that causes flaky crashes.

Bug: 588514727

[webauthn] Route enclave discovery via CreatePlatformDiscoveries

Instead of plumbing an enclave EventStream through FidoDiscoveryFactory
and gating discovery creation on a separate consider_enclave flag in
FidoRequestHandlerBase::InitDiscoveries(), construct
EnclaveAuthenticatorDiscovery directly in
ChromeAuthenticatorRequestDelegate::ConfigureGpm() (unifying GPM
availability checks across regular and passkey upgrade requests) and
return it from CreatePlatformDiscoveries().

Attach additional_discoveries unconditionally in
FidoRequestHandlerBase::InitDiscoveries() so embedder-provided platform
discoveries (both EnclaveAuthenticatorDiscovery and the upcoming
GpmBrowserAuthenticatorDiscovery) still run on Windows when
MaybeCreateWinWebAuthnApiDiscovery() succeeds and InitDiscoveries()
erases kInternal from available_transports, as well as during passkey
upgrade requests that clear available_transports.

Removing consider_enclave is a no-op: FidoRequestHandlerBase's
constructor (used by GetAssertionRequestHandler) hardcoded it to true,
and MakeCredentialRequestHandler checked
request_.authenticator_attachment != kCrossPlatform even though
request_.authenticator_attachment is always kAny at construction time
(the RP's requested attachment is on options_.authenticator_attachment).
Excluding GPM when kCrossPlatform is requested (or when kInternal is
disallowed on GetAssertion) was already enforced in
AuthenticatorCommonImpl via discover_enclave, passed as
browser_provided_passkeys_available to ConfigureDiscoveries() to gate
ConfigureGpm().

Also fold FidoDiscoveryFactory::set_network_context_factory() into
set_cable_data(), its only remaining consumer.

Bug: 570170982

[performance_manager] Add UMA metrics for restored tab loading outcomes

This CL adds SessionRestore.BackgroundTabLoading.TabCount{Outcome},
recording the number of tabs per outcome in a restore wave:
- (no suffix): Total scheduled tabs (sum of the outcomes below).
- .LoadedByPolicy: Load initiated by BackgroundTabLoadingPolicy.
- .AlreadyLoading: Already loading when scheduled (e.g. active tab).
- .LoadedExternally: Started loading while queued (e.g. user activated).
- .ClosedBeforeLoading: Closed before loading started.
- .SkippedForMaxTabs: Skipped at the 20-tab cap.
- .SkippedForFreeMemory: Skipped with < 150 MiB free physical memory.
- .SkippedForTabAge: Skipped because not visible for > 30 days.
- .StoppedForMemoryPressure: Dropped on moderate+ memory pressure.

A restore wave starts when ScheduleLoadForRestoredTabs() is called
while no restored tabs are pending, includes any overlapping calls, and
ends when all tracked restored tabs finish loading (or at shutdown).

These histograms will be used to assess the impact of planned changes
to BackgroundTabLoadingPolicy to reduce Chrome's total Private Memory
Footprint.

2f8dfaf61944858204db159e663e9c36ebb08148
parent
64a5dbcc73680d19e9a3a261d12ae663b8dd69c0 [ diff ] 
omnibox: Fill the context menu before the AI Mode page loads

The classic popup's "+" button opens a menu of tabs, inputs and tools
that acts on the AI Mode popup, and creates that popup's page if it
doesn't exist yet. The menu read those items from the page's
searchbox handler, which only exists once the page has loaded, so a
menu that created the page came up without them. Once the AI Mode
popup is created on demand, that happens on the first "+" click in
every window.

Read the input state model the handler will use, which exists before
the page loads. Move the tests that forced eager creation to work
around this back to their base fixtures, except on Mac, where the
classic popup then stays open after a tab is picked.

Bug: 570224989

Reland "[webnn] Run WebNN worker on a dedicated base::Thread with GPU priorities"

This is a reland of commit f743900eff04685a9f7a542a849ee816ae886369

Fix for TSan data race (crbug.com/587951569):
ExportTensorAsyncRejectedWhenSyncPointGraphValidationDisabled (and
InRendererConcurrentReadsAndWriteTest on Windows) declared a local
ScopedFeatureList inside TEST_F, after WebNNTestEnvironment was already
constructed. With WebNN's worker moved off ThreadPool onto a dedicated
base::Thread, ScopedFeatureList::Reset()'s ParallelExecutionFence no
longer pauses WebNNWorkerThread, so ~ScopedFeatureList() raced with
Mojo pipe teardown on WebNNWorkerThread reading FeatureList. Move the
disabled features into WebNNTensorImplBackendTest's fixture-level
ScopedFeatureList (constructed before WebNNTestEnvironment), and fix
the member declaration order in WebNNContextProviderImplTest as well.

Original change's description:
> [webnn] Run WebNN worker on a dedicated base::Thread with GPU priorities
>
> gpu::Scheduler's TaskQueues need a thread whose SequenceManager has GPU
> priority settings, which ThreadPool SingleThreadTaskRunners don't have.
> Run WebNNContextProviderImpl's worker on a dedicated base::Thread
> configured with gpu::CreateGpuSequenceManagerSettings(), and
> create/destroy its scheduler sequence on that thread so the sequence's
> lifetime matches where its tasks run.
>
> Bug: 586561155
> Change-Id: I5a65b81a4ec9f30f657fd61e641864e212c220e5
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8727869
> Reviewed-by: Reilly Grant <reillyg@chromium.org>
> Commit-Queue: Etienne Pierre-Doray <etiennep@chromium.org>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1748322}

Bug: 586561155, 587951569
Test: services_unittests --gtest_filter="*WebNN*" (TSan and default)

[variations] Support hybrid PQ seed signatures and key rotation

Pin three hybrid ECDSA P-256 + ML-DSA-44 Variations seed signing
keys (Key IDs 1, 2, and 3) matching Tink's
COMPOSITE_SIGN_MLDSA_44_ECDSA_P256_TINK_RAW profile, and wire the
client-advertised key protocol across C++, Android, WebView, and
iOS first-run fetchers.

- Send X-Seed-Key-Ids (1,2,3,0 while legacy is still trusted;
  1,2,3 once a new-key seed is stored) on seed fetch requests.
- Reuse X-Seed-Signature with a <key_id>:<base64_sig> prefix for
  hybrid signatures, keeping legacy Base64 signatures supported
  until a valid new-key seed is verified and stored.
- Verify hybrid ML-DSA-44 + ECDSA P-256 signatures via BoringSSL
  and persist kVariationsLegacySeedKeyDistrusted in Local State
  once a valid new-key seed is stored (flushing both the SeedFile
  and Local State on transition, and healing on load). On Chrome,
  subsequent legacy-only seeds are rejected while preserving the
  previous valid seed.
- Fix kIdenticalToSafeSeedSentinel aliasing to require matching
  signatures as well as seed bytes.
- WebView persists only kVariationsLegacySeedKeyDistrusted per
  embedding app and re-imports the seed from the WebView service
  on every startup. VariationsSeedFetcher ignores legacy-only
  responses when the stored seed already has a hybrid prefix; if
  an app that distrusts legacy is still handed a legacy-only seed
  (e.g. after a WebView provider switch), it rejects the seed and
  runs with no variations until a hybrid seed is fetched.

Bug: 588691630

[LiveCaption] Re-enable Live Caption browsertests on Linux

This CL re-enables `LiveCaptionControllerTest.OnSodaError` and
`LiveCaptionSpeechRecognitionHostTest.DestroysWithoutCrashing`.

`LiveCaptionControllerTest.OnSodaError` was disabled in
https://crrev.com/c/4951015 (https://crbug.com/40936746) due to test
expectation failures following the introduction of multi-language Live
Caption. Originally, SODA installation errors toggled
`prefs::kLiveCaptionEnabled` off. With multi-language support finalized
in https://crrev.com/c/7037377, the controller no longer turns off the
preference upon error, delegating user-facing error reporting to
`CaptionsHandler` in the Settings WebUI. This CL updates the assertions
to verify that `prefs::kLiveCaptionEnabled` remains enabled when SODA
install errors occur.

`LiveCaptionSpeechRecognitionHostTest.DestroysWithoutCrashing` was
disabled in https://crrev.com/c/2940284 (https://crbug.com/40184759) due
to flakiness across platforms. The test navigated to external
`http://www.google.com`, which triggered unmocked network safety aborts
in browser test environments. This CL switches navigation to use
`embedded_test_server()->GetURL("/title1.html")` and asserts that
`new_frame_host` differs from `frame_host`.

Tested on Linux.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

Fixed: 40936746
Fixed: 40184759
